- **uat-diagnoser**: uat (Use Agents for Testing) DIAGNOSER. Monitors and diagnoses the result after uat-injector sends a goal — observes topics/echo/hz, lifecycle state, TF, logs and exit codes, then isolates the root cause using the symptom→cause→skill routing table (QoS mismatch, undeclared parameter, lifecycle failure, TF extrapolation, goal rejected, plugin load, deadlock, etc.). - **uat-fixer**: uat (Use Agents for Testing) FIXER. Takes a uat-diagnoser diagnosis and applies a minimal, surgical fix guided by the matching project skill, respecting Clean Architecture layers. Does NOT spawn other agents — for build/CMake or architectural sub-problems it names the relevant uab agent/skill (cpp-build-resolver, clean-arch-architect, ros2-lifecycle, ...) so the MAIN SESSION can route there and re-run uat-runner to confirm. - **uat-injector**: uat (Use Agents for Testing) INJECTOR. - **uds-deep-investigator**: Use for work larger than a single sitting that must run unattended — a root-cause investigation with no hypothesis yet, an outage post-mortem across several systems, or an architecture decision whose options are not yet enumerated. ([AsiaOstrich/universal-dev-standards](https://github.com/AsiaOstrich/universal-dev-standards/blob/HEAD/integrations/claude-code/.claude/agents/uds-deep-investigator.md) · ★85)
- **uds-feature-implementer**: Use to implement a feature whose goal and integration points are already defined, refactor a module toward a stated target structure, or write integration tests for a specified subsystem. Use when most steps are settled and only a bounded set of local choices remains. Do NOT use when the target structure itself is still undecided — that is a higher tier. ([AsiaOstrich/universal-dev-standards](https://github.com/AsiaOstrich/universal-dev-standards/blob/HEAD/integrations/claude-code/.claude/agents/uds-feature-implementer.md) · ★85)
- **uds-mechanical-edit**: Use when the steps are already written out and the only work left is applying them — a mechanical rename across known files, a version bump, adding an export the spec already names, fixing a typo. Use proactively for any edit where literal following is exactly what is wanted. Do NOT use when the target structure is still undecided, or when the change requires a design judgment. ([AsiaOstrich/universal-dev-standards](https://github.com/AsiaOstrich/universal-dev-standards/blob/HEAD/integrations/claude-code/.claude/agents/uds-mechanical-edit.md) · ★85)

- **ufo-connector**: Cross-axis pattern finder (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). ([VeigaPunk/shokunin](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/shokunin/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ufo-connector.md) · ★1)
- **ufo-critic**: Adversarial approach review against the round's declared axes and invariants (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). Attacks the approach, not the task owner; hunts hidden regressions, duplicate authorities, and premature saturation. ([VeigaPunk/shokunin](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/shokunin/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ufo-critic.md) · ★1)
- **ufo-distiller**: Deduplicates complete proposals before per-axis judgment (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). Normalizes without changing meaning; dedupes by behavior and evidence, never wording. ([VeigaPunk/shokunin](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/shokunin/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ufo-distiller.md) · ★1)
- **ufo-executor**: Scoped implementation with behavioral evidence (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). Implements exactly the assigned move and runs the narrow behavioral check; the round's per-axis verdicts alone admit it. ([VeigaPunk/shokunin](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/shokunin/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ufo-executor.md) · ★1)
- **ufo-labrat**: Bounded empirical probe for one falsifiable hypothesis (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). Cheapest possible run; read-only against the repo; measured results distinguished from inference. ([VeigaPunk/shokunin](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/shokunin/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ufo-labrat.md) · ★1)
- **ufo-mutation-tester**: Mutate-run-revert probe for missing behavioral coverage (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). Challenges one claimed invariant with one plausible defect in an isolated scratch copy. ([VeigaPunk/shokunin](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/shokunin/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ufo-mutation-tester.md) · ★1)
- **ufo-planner**: Round planner seat (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). Data walk first, then the smallest end-to-end skeleton with one representative case and its verification gate. - **ufo-revenger**: Reverse-engineers undocumented local behavior and original intent (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). ([VeigaPunk/shokunin](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/shokunin/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ufo-revenger.md) · ★1)
- **ufo-reviewer**: Surgical correctness review of a bounded change (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). Reviews the actual diff and changed behavior; findings are severity-ranked recommendations, never admissions. ([VeigaPunk/shokunin](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/shokunin/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ufo-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **ufo-scout**: Read-only codebase research for one bounded unknown (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). Search before reading; separates observed facts, inference, and unresolved gaps. - **ufo-sentinel**: Adversarial security review of a bounded change (UFO-FSD role, Kimi-adapted). Inspects trust boundaries and failure modes; prefers exploit scenarios over generic hardening advice.
